# Metadata review of 250 highest-scoring recommendations with gaps

Selected before this review by unrounded repository score, then title. A gap means an unknown edition length, Amazon aggregate, or reference-page extent. This is a fixed 250-book cohort; new O15 deductions can change its ranks.

| Field | Added or corrected | Still unknown in cohort |
|---|---:|---:|
| Pages | 50 added; 3 corrected | 1 |
| Amazon ratings | 139 | 20 |
| Reference pages | 121 | 128 |
| Purchase links | 36 | — |

Library requests were cached and spaced at least 1.5 seconds apart. Amazon Australia requests were spaced at least 20 seconds apart. German and US product probes returned access challenges and were not retried. Amazon counts identify one product and one marketplace; they are dated snapshots. Unknown counts retain O15=0. Content assessments, eligibility decisions, original years and other deductions are preserved. Reference counts cover documented notes or bibliography ranges, exclude the index, and can use a different edition from total pages. Confidence: high for directly verified Amazon aggregates, with any identity conflict stated per book; moderate for catalog and contents-derived counts. Three pre-existing wrong-work page records are corrected: Famine, The Wheel, and Science and Civilisation in China Volume 4, Part 2. The edition audit is saved in data/top250_261023/edition-audit-reviewed.json.
